===== _commentary/v16/work/s050/surah.r2/channels.md =====
(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s050/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s050 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1: Joined Forms and Re-Fitted Creation
- Semantic invariant: A whole becomes intelligible through the fitting, joining, and covering operations that give it form.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:6 presents the sky as built, adorned, and without fissures, while 50:15 frames renewed creation as the object of confusion.
- Surprising reach: The lexical movement from construction to tailoring makes renewed creation resemble the replacement of a worn covering, not the recovery of an unstructured mass.

#### Subchannel A. The Joined and Adorned Canopy
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Parts are joined into a raised covering, beautified, and inspected for gaps.
- Active motifs: building by joining parts `quranic:root_000156:B001/m01`; the sky as what rises and covers `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; beautifying a formed object `quranic:root_000660:B002/m01`; a cleft or gap in a whole `quranic:root_001139:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:6 `بَنَيْنَٰهَا`, `ٱلسَّمَآءِ`, `زَيَّنَّٰهَا`, `فُرُوجٍ`
- Synthesis: The sky is not merely a large object overhead. It is a fitted canopy whose coherence depends on joined components, visible finish, and the absence of ruptures. Adornment and gaplessness therefore operate as evidence that fabrication has reached a stable whole.

#### Subchannel B. Creation as a Replaced Garment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A form is measured and made, an old covering is displaced, and a new garment supplies the image of renewed embodiment.
- Active motifs: measuring before cutting or forming `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; creation as fabrication `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; a worn garment `quranic:root_000434:B009/m01`; a new garment `quranic:root_000227:B004/m01`; clothing as a covering `quranic:root_001341:B001/m01`; confusion produced by mixing `quranic:root_001341:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:15 `بِٱلْخَلْقِ ٱلْأَوَّلِ`, `خَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ`, `لَبْسٍ`
- Synthesis: The question of a new creation acquires a material process: measure, fabricate, cover, and replace. The same lexical field that names confusion also supplies clothing, so perplexity about re-creation is reframed as failure to recognize that a formed covering can be renewed.

### 2. P1: Delivered Water, Layered Yield, and Emergence
- Semantic invariant: What descends as a delivered resource is stored and transformed into successive visible forms of life.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:9-11 runs from blessed water through gardens, grain, palms, provision, revived land, and emergence.
- Surprising reach: Resurrection is pressured by a production chain rather than a loose nature comparison: delivery becomes germination, ordered layering, harvest, and outward passage.

#### Subchannel A. Rain as Delivered Provision
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water is sent down as a supplied stock whose retention enables growth and whose end use is provision.
- Active motifs: descent from above `quranic:root_001492:B001/m01`; delivery or sending down `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; water as a material resource `quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`; supplying by water `quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`; retained water that supports growth `quranic:root_000109:B003/m01`; rainfall functioning as provision `quranic:root_000560:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:9 `نَزَّلْنَا`, `مَآءً`, `مُبَٰرَكًا`; 50:11 `رِّزْقًا`
- Synthesis: Descent is also delivery. The water arrives as usable material, is held rather than lost, and is converted into provision. Blessing is thus materialized as the capacity of a supplied resource to remain productive after arrival.

#### Subchannel B. Seed, Garden, and Harvest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Planting and emergence turn seed into screened, vigorous vegetation and finally into a cut yield.
- Active motifs: plants emerging `quranic:root_001465:B001/m01`; planting `quranic:root_001465:B002/m01`; a tree-screened garden `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; entwined vigorous vegetation `quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`; seed or grain `quranic:root_000286:B001/m01`; harvesting `quranic:root_000327:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:9 `فَأَنۢبَتْنَا`, `جَنَّٰتٍ`, `حَبَّ`, `ٱلْحَصِيدِ`
- Synthesis: The scene is a complete agricultural conversion: seed is placed, growth comes outward, vegetation thickens into an enclosing garden, and the crop reaches harvest. The visible yield records several hidden operations that preceded it.

#### Subchannel C. The Palm as a Vertical Layered Assembly
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A palm rises to full height while its fruit-bearing growth emerges and stacks in ordered layers.
- Active motifs: the palm tree `quranic:root_001483:B001/m01`; height and complete growth `quranic:root_000117:B001/m01`; emergence of a spathe or plant growth `quranic:root_000945:B005/m01`; orderly stacking `quranic:root_001515:B001/m01`; natural layers `quranic:root_001515:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:10 `ٱلنَّخْلَ`, `بَاسِقَٰتٍ`, `طَلْعٌ`, `نَّضِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: The palm concentrates growth into an architectural sequence: rise, emergence, and layered loading. Its fruit is not an undifferentiated abundance but an ordered assembly borne by a completed vertical form.

#### Subchannel D. Dead Ground Re-Enters the Cycle
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A land deprived of life receives water, becomes living, and sends what was hidden outward in a recurrent return.
- Active motifs: death or loss of life `quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`; dead or unworked land `quranic:root_001454:B003/m01`; earth living through rain and plants `quranic:root_000383:B002/m01`; outward passage or emergence `quranic:root_000400:B001/m01`; recurrent return associated with rain and water `quranic:root_000544:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:3 `مِتْنَا`, `رَجْعٌ`; 50:11 `بَلْدَةً مَّيْتًا`, `أَحْيَيْنَا`, `ٱلْخُرُوجُ`
- Synthesis: The land passes from interrupted productivity back into active output. Its revival is a recurrent mechanism in which water restores a latent capacity and the concealed product crosses outward, giving emergence a concrete ecological grammar.

### 3. P1: Material Loss and Informational Retention
- Semantic invariant: Physical matter can diminish while its determining knowledge remains preserved.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:3-4 juxtaposes dust and what the earth subtracts with divine knowledge and a preserving book.
- Surprising reach: Earthly decomposition and written or remembered conservation divide the person into a lossy material trace and a lossless determining record.

#### Subchannel A. Earth as a Decomposing Medium
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The dead body becomes soil while the earth consumes, decays, and subtracts from its material form.
- Active motifs: loss or subtraction `quranic:root_001542:B001/m01`; an earth-creature eating material `quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`; a decaying or suppurating lesion `quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`; dust or soil `quranic:root_000178:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:3 `تُرَابًا`; 50:4 `تَنقُصُ ٱلْأَرْضُ`
- Synthesis: The earth is an active decomposing medium, not just the body's destination. Consumption, lesion-like decay, and subtraction describe several modes by which a prior form becomes materially unavailable.

#### Subchannel B. The Preserved Determining Record
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Knowledge of the lost form is fixed in writing, guarded, and retained as if in memory.
- Active motifs: disclosed knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; written text `quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`; a fixed decree `quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`; guarding or preserving `quranic:root_000342:B001/m01`; retention in memory `quranic:root_000342:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:4 `عَلِمْنَا`, `كِتَٰبٌ حَفِيظٌ`
- Synthesis: The record answers material subtraction with another kind of persistence. Writing fixes what decay disperses, while guarding and memory prevent informational loss; restoration can therefore proceed from preserved determination rather than surviving matter.

### 4. P1: Covered Truth and Tangled Judgment
- Semantic invariant: Rejection works by covering what is real and then inhabiting the disorder produced by that concealment.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:2 and 50:5 name rejection, denial of truth, and a tangled affair, while 50:15 names confusion about new creation.
- Surprising reach: The rejecting mind resembles both a covered object and a garment whose materials have been mixed until its form is hard to distinguish.

#### Subchannel A. Truth Covered and Called False
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A real disclosure arrives, is covered from view, and is actively recast as false.
- Active motifs: covering something `quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`; concealing or rejecting truth `quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`; truth or reality `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; establishing or showing truth `quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`; accusing or denying as a lie `quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:2 `ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ`; 50:5 `كَذَّبُوا بِٱلْحَقِّ`
- Synthesis: Rejection is a two-stage operation: the real is first covered and then redescribed as unreal. The lie is therefore not an independent object but a verbal cover laid over an arriving disclosure.

#### Subchannel B. The Entangled Affair
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Mixed components lose a stable arrangement and become a tangled, wavering whole.
- Active motifs: a mixed or disordered affair `quranic:root_001411:B004/m01`; instability `quranic:root_001411:B005/m01`; an entangled branch or crooked arrow `quranic:root_001411:B006/m01`; confusion produced by mixing `quranic:root_001341:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:5 `أَمْرٍ مَّرِيجٍ`; 50:15 `لَبْسٍ`
- Synthesis: Confusion is given shape as an object whose parts no longer align: branches interlace, an arrow loses its straight course, and mixed fabric obscures its construction. Cognitive instability is thus rendered as defective arrangement.

### 5. P1: Collective Bonds and a Message Coming Due
- Semantic invariant: Communities are held together by affiliations, but their common response to a dispatched message gives those bonds a shared outcome.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:12-14 names peoples, companions, brethren, messengers, collective denial, and the warning that became due.
- Surprising reach: The people of the Rass add a downward fixing or pit image to the social field, while successive dispatched groups turn repeated denial into an accumulating obligation.

#### Subchannel A. Peoples, Companions, and Tethered Kin
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Groups cohere through peoplehood, companionship, and kinship, with the Rass adding a fixed or excavated place to collective identity.
- Active motifs: a human group or people `quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`; companionship `quranic:root_000844:B001/m01`; brotherhood or kinship `quranic:root_000020:B001/m01`; a tether or binding bond `quranic:root_000020:B002/m01`; being fixed in place `quranic:root_000562:B001/m01`; digging or placing in a pit or grave `quranic:root_000562:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:12 `أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلرَّسِّ`; 50:13 `إِخْوَٰنُ لُوطٍ`; 50:14 `قَوْمُ تُبَّعٍ`
- Synthesis: The named collectives are not interchangeable labels. Companionship, tethered kinship, and attachment to a fixed or excavated place provide different mechanisms of group cohesion, all of which can carry a common stance across members.

#### Subchannel B. Dispatch, Denial, and Fulfilled Warning
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Messages are successively dispatched to groups, collectively rejected, and converted by that response into a warning that is now due.
- Active motifs: dispatch or sending `quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`; a messenger or message carrier `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; successive groups `quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`; accusing or denying as a lie `quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`; becoming due or obligatory `quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`; a threat or warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:12 `كَذَّبَتْ`; 50:14 `كَذَّبَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْأَيْكَةِ وَقَوْمُ تُبَّعٍ كُلٌّ كَذَّبَ ٱلرُّسُلَ فَحَقَّ وَعِيدِ`
- Synthesis: Dispatch creates a social obligation to answer. Because rejection recurs across successive groups, the warning does not remain a remote possibility; the communities' repeated response is the operation by which it comes due.

### 6. P2: Interior Speech Rendered as Evidence
- Semantic invariant: Speech moves from inaccessible interior motion to received, guarded, and presentation-ready evidence.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:16-18 proceeds from self-whispering and bodily nearness to paired receivers and every utterance being met by a prepared watcher.
- Surprising reach: The person is pictured as both a bound interior through which faint signals move and a source that ejects verbal objects into an already staffed reception system.

#### Subchannel A. Whisper Inside the Bound Body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Faint self-talk arises within intention and is known at a nearness figured by binding cords and the life vein.
- Active motifs: hidden self-talk `quranic:root_001651:B001/m01`; a faint sound `quranic:root_001651:B002/m01`; mind or intention within the self `quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`; disclosed knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; nearness `quranic:root_001212:B001/m01`; a binding rope `quranic:root_000291:B001/m01`; bodily cords or veins `quranic:root_000291:B004/m01`; the life vein or jugular `quranic:root_001640:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:16 `تُوَسْوِسُ بِهِۦ نَفْسُهُ`, `نَعْلَمُ`, `أَقْرَبُ`, `حَبْلِ ٱلْوَرِيدِ`
- Synthesis: Interior speech is faint but not placeless. It occupies the intending self within a body imagined through cords and a life-bearing vein; divine nearness reaches the signal before it has become public sound.

#### Subchannel B. Paired Receivers at Their Posts
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Two receivers take in material while seated in opposed watch positions and maintaining guard.
- Active motifs: receiving or meeting what arrives `quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`; taking or learning speech `quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`; sitting in watch `quranic:root_001244:B005/m01`; watchful waiting `quranic:root_000584:B001/m01`; guarding `quranic:root_000584:B002/m01`; the right side `quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`; the left side `quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:17 `يَتَلَقَّى ٱلْمُتَلَقِّيَانِ`, `عَنِ ٱلْيَمِينِ وَعَنِ ٱلشِّمَالِ قَعِيدٌ`; 50:18 `رَقِيبٌ`
- Synthesis: Reception is spatially organized. Opposed posts create complete lateral coverage, while sitting, waiting, and guarding turn the reception of speech into a continuous office rather than an occasional intervention.

#### Subchannel C. Utterance Ejected into Readiness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An utterance crosses the mouth as an expelled object and immediately encounters prepared custody.
- Active motifs: spoken utterance `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; ejecting something from the mouth `quranic:root_001364:B001/m01`; verbalizing `quranic:root_001364:B002/m01`; being present and prepared `quranic:root_000978:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:18 `يَلْفِظُ مِن قَوْلٍ`, `عَتِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: Speech becomes evidentiary at the moment it leaves the mouth. Lexically, verbalization is expulsion; what exits the speaker as an object meets a witness already present and ready to receive it.

### 7. P2: Constriction, Signal, and Compelled Procession
- Semantic invariant: Agency narrows through a sequence in which death constricts, a signal initiates the appointed event, and each self is escorted into testimony.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:19-21 moves from death's intoxication through the horn blast to the arrival of every self with driver and witness.
- Surprising reach: Breath, which ordinarily sustains life, is redirected into a horn whose signal begins the procession of selves no longer controlling their route.

#### Subchannel A. Death as Intoxication and Choking Narrowness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Death arrives as a truth that overwhelms sobriety, tightens passage, and defeats attempted deviation.
- Active motifs: arrival `quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`; intoxication or loss of sobriety `quranic:root_000725:B001/m01`; choking constriction `quranic:root_000725:B005/m01`; loss of life `quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`; truth or reality `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; deviation or avoidance `quranic:root_000376:B001/m01`; an angled protrusion that turns aside `quranic:root_000376:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:19 `جَآءَتْ سَكْرَةُ ٱلْمَوْتِ بِٱلْحَقِّ`, `مِنْهُ تَحِيدُ`
- Synthesis: Death constrains both perception and movement. Intoxication removes lucid control, choking narrows the available passage, and deviation becomes only an angled attempt to turn away from an arriving reality.

#### Subchannel B. Breath Converted into an Appointed Signal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Breath is sent into a horn, producing the signal that makes the threatened day operational.
- Active motifs: sending wind or breath into something `quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`; a horn `quranic:root_000891:B004/m01`; a catastrophic day `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`; a threat or warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:20 `نُفِخَ فِى ٱلصُّورِ`, `يَوْمُ ٱلْوَعِيدِ`
- Synthesis: The warning passes from speech into mechanism. A directed breath activates an instrument, and its sound converts an announced day into a present event.

#### Subchannel C. The Self Driven Under Witness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Each living self arrives under directional force, accompanied by an escort who drives and another who bears testimony.
- Active motifs: the living self `quranic:root_001533:B011/m01`; driving or herding `quranic:root_000762:B001/m01`; following in one track `quranic:root_000762:B012/m01`; a present witness `quranic:root_000822:B001/m01`; testimony `quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`; arrival `quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:21 `جَآءَتْ كُلُّ نَفْسٍ مَّعَهَا سَآئِقٌ وَشَهِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: The self is no longer an autonomous traveler. Driving supplies route and force, while testimony supplies an accompanying account; movement and evidence converge in one escorted arrival.

### 8. P2: Veil Removal and Edge-Sharp Sight
- Semantic invariant: Disclosure removes a cognitive covering and transforms sight from passive capacity into penetrating discrimination.
- Surface relation: direct; all active anchors occur in 50:22, where heedlessness is a covering that is removed and sight becomes iron-sharp.
- Surprising reach: Heedlessness resembles both unmarked terrain and an ignorance veil, while awakened sight acquires the cutting function of a metal edge.

#### Subchannel A. Heedlessness as Unmarked, Covered Terrain
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A subject moves without vigilant marks under a cover that blocks knowledgeable perception.
- Active motifs: heedlessness outside vigilance `quranic:root_001097:B001/m01`; being left unmarked or unwritten `quranic:root_001097:B002/m01`; unmarked land or path `quranic:root_001097:B006/m01`; a cover `quranic:root_001095:B001/m01`; a veil of ignorance `quranic:root_001095:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:22 `فِى غَفْلَةٍ`, `غِطَآءَكَ`
- Synthesis: Heedlessness is not mere inattention. It is a condition in which the route bears no orienting marks and a covering prevents recognition, combining navigational and cognitive deprivation.

#### Subchannel B. Uncovering Produces a Cutting Gaze
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A cover is removed, what was hidden is exposed, and vision takes on the penetrative quality of sharpened iron.
- Active motifs: removing a cover `quranic:root_001302:B001/m01`; exposing a hidden flaw `quranic:root_001302:B002/m01`; eyesight `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; inner insight `quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`; hard iron `quranic:root_000002:B004/m01`; a cutting or penetrating edge, including sharp sight `quranic:root_000002:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:22 `فَكَشَفْنَا`, `بَصَرُكَ`, `حَدِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: Uncovering does more than restore ordinary visibility. The exposed field is met by a gaze hardened into an edge, joining physical acuity and inward recognition as one capacity to penetrate what the veil had concealed.

### 9. P2: Adjudicating Withholding, Rivalry, and Responsibility
- Semantic invariant: Judgment separates distinct operations of culpability and fixes their consequences without allowing responsibility to be transferred.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:24-29 presents the accused profile, rival deity, companion's defense, prior warning, fixed speech, and denial of injustice.
- Surprising reach: The root of reception in 50:17 becomes throwing in 50:24 and 50:26: a system that first receives speech later casts the responsible party into judgment.

#### Subchannel A. The Withholding Transgressor
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Stubborn rejection becomes a barrier that withholds beneficial good, crosses bounds, and sustains suspicion.
- Active motifs: concealing or rejecting truth `quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`; stubborn deviation or resistance `quranic:root_001052:B001/m01`; withholding a gift `quranic:root_001448:B001/m01`; a barrier `quranic:root_001448:B002/m01`; beneficial good `quranic:root_000452:B001/m01`; transgression or overstepping `quranic:root_000993:B001/m01`; doubt or suspicion `quranic:root_000616:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:24 `كَفَّارٍ عَنِيدٍ`; 50:25 `مَنَّاعٍ لِّلْخَيْرِ مُعْتَدٍ مُّرِيبٍ`
- Synthesis: The charges form one obstructive posture. Rejection resists incoming truth, withholding blocks outgoing good, transgression crosses the proper boundary, and suspicion supplies the mental state that maintains the barrier.

#### Subchannel B. A Rival Installed, Then the Accused Cast
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Another object of worship is assigned beside God, after which the responsible subject is thrown away into severe punishment.
- Active motifs: assigning or making into a state `quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`; an object of worship `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; another or a rival `quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`; throwing or casting `quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`; a discarded object `quranic:root_001372:B006/m01`; severe force `quranic:root_000782:B002/m01`; punishment `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:26 `جَعَلَ مَعَ ٱللَّهِ إِلَٰهًا ءَاخَرَ`, `أَلْقِيَاهُ فِى ٱلْعَذَابِ ٱلشَّدِيدِ`
- Synthesis: The scene reverses the accused's act. The person first assigns a rival to an illegitimate place and is then assigned the condition of a discarded object, cast under a force that cannot be resisted.

#### Subchannel C. The Bound Companion Disputes Causation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A companion joined to the accused contests causal responsibility, denying that it drove the other beyond bounds and locating the error in remote straying.
- Active motifs: binding things together `quranic:root_001221:B001/m01`; a companion `quranic:root_001221:B002/m01`; negotiation or disputed speech `quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`; causing or exceeding a limit `quranic:root_000937:B001/m01`; straying `quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`; disappearing or burial `quranic:root_000913:B002/m01`; distance `quranic:root_000131:B001/m01`; a litigant's dispute `quranic:root_000416:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:27 `قَالَ قَرِينُهُ`, `مَآ أَطْغَيْتُهُ`, `ضَلَٰلٍۭ بَعِيدٍ`; 50:28 `تَخْتَصِمُوا`
- Synthesis: Companionship supplies proximity but not transferable agency. The bound associate argues that causal overreach was absent and that the accused's path was already remotely astray; judgment therefore becomes a dispute over who moved whom across the limit.

#### Subchannel D. Prior Warning and Unalterable Verdict
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A warning has already been advanced, the judicial word remains fixed, and neither substitution nor deprivation of servants' rights is admitted.
- Active motifs: advancing or preceding `quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`; intending or approaching `quranic:root_001207:B010/m01`; a threat or warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`; an appointed time or place `quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`; spoken judgment `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; substitution `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; altering a form `quranic:root_000095:B002/m01`; putting a thing in the wrong place or time `quranic:root_000967:B004/m01`; withholding rights `quranic:root_000967:B008/m01`; a servant `quranic:root_000973:B001/m01`; worshipful obedience `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:28 `قَدَّمْتُ إِلَيْكُم بِٱلْوَعِيدِ`; 50:29 `مَا يُبَدَّلُ ٱلْقَوْلُ لَدَىَّ`, `بِظَلَّٰمٍ لِّلْعَبِيدِ`
- Synthesis: Finality rests on sequence and placement. Warning preceded verdict, so the judgment is neither surprise nor retroactive substitution; its immutability is paired with the claim that no servant's due is displaced or withheld.

### 10. P3: Capacity, Appetite, and Surplus
- Semantic invariant: Receptacles disclose their disposition through what they do with fullness and increase.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:30 gives Hell a voice that asks for more after being filled, while 50:31 and 50:35 bring the garden near and promise whatever is willed plus increase.
- Surprising reach: Hell speaks as a container whose state demands further input, whereas the garden's increase behaves as provision offered beyond desire rather than appetite seeking completion.

#### Subchannel A. The Filled Vessel That Still Requests More
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A vessel reaches fullness, speaks through its condition, and converts increase into an unsatisfied request.
- Active motifs: vessel fullness or capacity `quranic:root_001441:B001/m01`; increase or growth `quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`; requesting or bidding for more `quranic:root_000657:B004/m01`; a thing speaking by its state `quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:30 `ٱمْتَلَأْتِ`, `تَقُولُ`, `مِن مَّزِيدٍ`
- Synthesis: Fullness fails to terminate appetite. The vessel's condition itself becomes speech, and increase is grammatically recast as a request, exposing a capacity whose defining trait is not size but inability to conclude consumption.

#### Subchannel B. The Near Garden and Gift Beyond Desire
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A protected garden is brought near, receives the inhabitants' willing desire, and adds journey-like provision beyond what was requested.
- Active motifs: a tree-screened garden `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; approach or drawing near `quranic:root_000639:B001/m01`; nearness or rank `quranic:root_000639:B002/m01`; will or desire `quranic:root_000831:B002/m01`; increase or growth `quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`; provisions for a journey `quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:31 `أُزْلِفَتِ ٱلْجَنَّةُ`; 50:35 `مَا يَشَآءُونَ`, `مَزِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: Here increase is not demanded by an empty appetite. The destination approaches its guests, desire is fulfilled inside it, and the additional gift resembles provision supplied for an onward abundance even after arrival.

### 11. P3: Return into Guarded Permanence
- Semantic invariant: Repeated inward return is maintained until it resolves into secure, lasting habitation.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:32-34 moves from the returning guardian and an inwardly returning heart to entry in peace on the day of permanence.
- Surprising reach: The heart is both an inward organ and a turning mechanism; its recurrent orientation becomes the preparatory motion for entering a dwelling where turning no longer means instability.

#### Subchannel A. The Guarded Inward Return
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person repeatedly turns back, maintains vigilance before the unseen, and arrives with a heart oriented toward return.
- Active motifs: return to a home or refuge `quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`; guarding `quranic:root_000342:B001/m01`; maintaining something `quranic:root_000342:B003/m01`; vigilance `quranic:root_000342:B004/m01`; reverent fear `quranic:root_000413:B001/m01`; what is hidden from sight `quranic:root_001117:B001/m01`; the heart `quranic:root_001248:B001/m01`; turning a thing `quranic:root_001248:B004/m01`; a destination `quranic:root_001248:B005/m01`; recurrent return `quranic:root_001562:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:32 `أَوَّابٍ حَفِيظٍ`; 50:33 `خَشِىَ ٱلرَّحْمَٰنَ بِٱلْغَيْبِ`, `بِقَلْبٍ مُّنِيبٍ`
- Synthesis: Return is sustained rather than episodic. Guarding preserves the orientation, fear operates without visual possession, and the heart repeatedly turns toward its destination until inward movement becomes a stable disposition.

#### Subchannel B. Entry into Safe Permanence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The returning subject crosses inward into a protected chamber of peace and remains there without expiry.
- Active motifs: entering `quranic:root_000464:B001/m01`; an inner chamber or secret interior `quranic:root_000464:B003/m01`; safety or freedom from harm `quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`; peace `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`; permanence `quranic:root_000429:B001/m01`; clinging or staying `quranic:root_000429:B002/m01`; a settled inward thought `quranic:root_000429:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:34 `ٱدْخُلُوهَا بِسَلَٰمٍ`, `يَوْمُ ٱلْخُلُودِ`
- Synthesis: Entry completes the motion of return by placing the subject inside rather than merely near the destination. Peace supplies protected occupancy, and permanence turns repeated orientation into settled presence.

### 12. P3: Penetration, Resistance, and Effortless Assembly
- Semantic invariant: Passage through resistant space exposes the difference between limited human force and divine operations that incur no fatigue.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:36 shows powerful generations probing the lands without escape, 50:38 denies fatigue in cosmic creation, and 50:44 depicts rapid, easy gathering through split earth.
- Surprising reach: Human probing resembles exhausting movement through a wasteland, while the final opening of earth is itself a strenuous rupture yet produces light, compliant motion for those gathered.

#### Subchannel A. Exhaustive Probing Without Escape
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Strong generations range through lands, pierce routes in search of an outlet, exhaust their effort, and find no escape.
- Active motifs: destruction `quranic:root_001596:B001/m01`; exhausting effort `quranic:root_001596:B009/m01`; a generation `quranic:root_001221:B005/m01`; severe force `quranic:root_000782:B002/m01`; a violent grasp `quranic:root_000126:B001/m01`; roaming or searching lands `quranic:root_001539:B006/m01`; land or place `quranic:root_000148:B001/m01`; evasion or escape `quranic:root_000378:B001/m01`; no-exit confusion `quranic:root_000378:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:36 `أَهْلَكْنَا`, `قَرْنٍ`, `أَشَدُّ مِنْهُم بَطْشًا`, `فَنَقَّبُوا فِى ٱلْبِلَٰدِ`, `مَّحِيصٍ`
- Synthesis: Superior grasp does not produce an exit. The generations spend their force piercing and searching the land, but range only converts strength into exhaustion; the sought route folds back into a condition of no escape.

#### Subchannel B. Cosmic Fabrication Without Fatigue
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Heaven, earth, and their interval are measured and fabricated as a connected whole without fatigue touching the maker.
- Active motifs: measuring before forming `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; creation as fabrication `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; the sky as what rises and covers `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; earth as the lower field `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; an interval between things `quranic:root_000170:B002/m01`; connection across an interval `quranic:root_000170:B003/m01`; touching or affecting `quranic:root_001423:B004/m01`; fatigue `quranic:root_001360:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:38 `خَلَقْنَا ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ وَمَا بَيْنَهُمَا`, `مَا مَسَّنَا مِن لُّغُوبٍ`
- Synthesis: The cosmos is one fabricated span, extending from lower field to raised cover and including the relation between them. Its scale does not register as depletion: fatigue cannot even make contact with the act that establishes the connected whole.

#### Subchannel C. Split Earth and Light, Rapid Gathering
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Earth ruptures open under strain, yet the gathered bodies move out swiftly and with compliant ease.
- Active motifs: splitting or opening `quranic:root_000807:B001/m01`; a strenuous burden `quranic:root_000807:B003/m01`; speed `quranic:root_000698:B001/m01`; leading the first arrivals `quranic:root_000698:B002/m01`; driving or gathering a group to a destination `quranic:root_000324:B001/m01`; fine, sharp, or light movement `quranic:root_000324:B006/m01`; ease `quranic:root_001694:B001/m01`; light compliant movement `quranic:root_001694:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:44 `تَشَقَّقُ ٱلْأَرْضُ`, `سِرَاعًا`, `حَشْرٌ`, `يَسِيرٌ`
- Synthesis: The rupture belongs to the earth, not to a difficult evacuation. Once the enclosure splits, the gathered group moves as a light, rapidly led body; a burdensome opening yields an easy collective passage.

### 13. P3: Praise at Moving Boundaries
- Semantic invariant: Devotion is maintained through transitions of light, darkness, and bodily posture.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:39-40 places praise before sunrise and sunset, during the night, and after prostration.
- Surprising reach: Praise shares a root image with swimming or rapid motion, so repeated worship can accompany the sun's moving boundaries rather than merely mark static clock points.

#### Subchannel A. Praise Along the Solar Passage
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Patient praise accompanies the rising and withdrawal of the sun, moving with the two visible boundaries of daylight.
- Active motifs: restraining oneself in patience `quranic:root_000840:B001/m01`; worshipful praise `quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`; swimming or running motion `quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`; praise `quranic:root_000355:B001/m01`; sunrise or a rising luminary `quranic:root_000945:B001/m01`; the sun or daylight `quranic:root_000818:B001/m01`; sunset or the west `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`; distance or withdrawal `quranic:root_001077:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:39 `فَٱصْبِرْ`, `سَبِّحْ بِحَمْدِ`, `طُلُوعِ ٱلشَّمْسِ`, `غُرُوبِهَا`
- Synthesis: Patience holds the worshipper steady while praise tracks a moving luminary. Rising and withdrawal define not isolated instants but a passage through which devotion continues, with the swimming image giving praise its own sustained motion.

#### Subchannel B. Night and the Traces After Prostration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Praise continues into nocturnal action and resumes behind or after the bodily act of prostration.
- Active motifs: night `quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`; action performed at night `quranic:root_001392:B002/m01`; what comes behind or after `quranic:root_000458:B002/m01`; considering an outcome `quranic:root_000458:B006/m01`; prostration or submission `quranic:root_000675:B001/m01`; a place of prostration `quranic:root_000675:B002/m01`; a bodily trace or member `quranic:root_000675:B003/m01`; worshipful praise `quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:40 `مِنَ ٱلَّيْلِ فَسَبِّحْهُ`, `أَدْبَٰرَ ٱلسُّجُودِ`
- Synthesis: Worship extends on both temporal and bodily axes. Night changes the setting, prostration changes posture, and what follows the posture preserves its effect as praise continues in the traces left by submission.

### 14. P3: From Offered Hearing to the Unavoidable Call
- Semantic invariant: Hearing ranges from a deliberately offered receptive posture to an acoustic disclosure that compels emergence, while reminder itself remains noncoercive.
- Surface relation: direct; 50:37 describes casting one's hearing while present, 50:41-42 commands listening for the nearby caller and true cry, and 50:45 limits the reminder to those who fear the warning.
- Surprising reach: Attention is something the hearer casts forward, but the final cry reverses initiative by drawing the hidden outward; the Quranic reminder stands between these poles as forceful address without compulsion.

#### Subchannel A. The Heart Offers Its Hearing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A remembering heart actively casts its faculty of hearing forward while remaining present enough to understand and bear witness.
- Active motifs: re-presenting what was forgotten `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; a reminder `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; the heart `quranic:root_001248:B001/m01`; casting or offering `quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`; hearing `quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`; understanding or obeying what is heard `quranic:root_000741:B003/m01`; presence as a witness `quranic:root_000822:B001/m01`; testimony `quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:37 `لَذِكْرَىٰ`, `قَلْبٌ`, `أَلْقَى ٱلسَّمْعَ`, `شَهِيدٌ`
- Synthesis: Reception is an act, not a vacant condition. The heart projects hearing toward the reminder, comprehension turns sound into response, and presence makes the event witnessable within the listener.

#### Subchannel B. The Nearby Caller and Outward-Producing Cry
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A caller raises sound from a near station; the cry becomes an inevitable disclosure that brings what was hidden outward.
- Active motifs: a raised call `quranic:root_001486:B002/m01`; an appearance resembling a call `quranic:root_001486:B008/m01`; nearness `quranic:root_001212:B001/m01`; a place or station `quranic:root_001332:B002/m01`; a high voice `quranic:root_000895:B001/m01`; a terrifying cry `quranic:root_000895:B002/m01`; truth or reality `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; becoming due or inevitable `quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`; outward passage `quranic:root_000400:B001/m01`; bringing the hidden out `quranic:root_000400:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:41 `يُنَادِ ٱلْمُنَادِ مِن مَّكَانٍ قَرِيبٍ`; 50:42 `يَسْمَعُونَ ٱلصَّيْحَةَ بِٱلْحَقِّ`, `يَوْمُ ٱلْخُرُوجِ`
- Synthesis: The call joins location, sound, and result. Nearness removes acoustic distance, the raised voice becomes a terrifying cry, and its truth is measured by what it accomplishes: hidden bodies cross outward into emergence.

#### Subchannel C. Reminder Without Compulsion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Recitation gathers words into a reminder that awakens fear of warning without forcing the hearer.
- Active motifs: coercion or compulsion `quranic:root_000216:B003/m01`; gathering into a collected text `quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`; reading or reciting `quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`; a reminder `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; fear `quranic:root_000447:B001/m01`; awakening fear `quranic:root_000447:B002/m01`; a threat or warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:45 `بِجَبَّارٍ`, `فَذَكِّرْ بِٱلْقُرْءَانِ`, `يَخَافُ وَعِيدِ`
- Synthesis: Recitation forms a collected verbal address, but its efficacy is not mechanical domination. It re-presents the warning so that fear awakens in a receptive hearer, preserving a distinction between compelling attention and coercing assent.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1: Gathered Recitation and Re-Presented Reminder
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Words are gathered into recitation, carried as warning, and used to bring obscured knowledge back before the listener.
- Active motifs: gathering into a collected text `quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`; reading or reciting `quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`; re-presenting what was forgotten `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; a reminder `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; a warning that awakens caution `quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:1 `ٱلْقُرْءَانِ`; 50:2 `مُّنذِرٌ`; 50:8 `ذِكْرَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Recitation first gathers dispersed words into an ordered verbal object. As warning and reminder, that object does not merely transmit new information; it makes a neglected reality present again so that caution can awaken.

### S2. Whole-surah: Well, Bucket, and Poured Water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rainwater is retained in a rock hollow or well, raised in a handled bucket on a standing apparatus, balanced under load, and poured into a receiving basin.
- Active motifs: a rock hollow or newly dug well that retains rainwater `quranic:root_000434:B011/m01`; an elongated bucket with one loop `quranic:root_000737:B011/m01`; a bucket loop, side, or wooden support that balances its load `quranic:root_000741:B008/m01`; a large complete bucket or water carrier `quranic:root_001077:B002/m01`; water spilling from well buckets and filling a basin `quranic:root_001077:B003/m01`; a standing part of the well pulley apparatus `quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:12 `قَوْمُ`; 50:15 `خَلْقٍ`; 50:34 `سَلَٰمٍ`; 50:37 `ٱلسَّمْعَ`; 50:39 `غُرُوبِهَا`
- Synthesis: The cluster turns water provision into a complete handling mechanism: descent is caught, stored, lifted, balanced, and poured. This materializes the surah's rain-to-revival reading by showing how a delivered resource remains available for use, and it gives reception a tool-like precision rather than treating abundance as an unstructured flood.

### S3. Whole-surah: Gestation, Birth, and Postpartum Passage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hidden womb gathers a fetus, nourishment begins to prepare before delivery, birth sends the child and blood outward, and the womb passes through afterbirth and postpartum pain.
- Active motifs: the concealed reproductive organ or womb `quranic:root_000383:B011/m01`; a womb gathering a fetus or blood `quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`; milk or colostrum descending before birth `quranic:root_000117:B003/m01`; childbirth, the newborn, and postpartum blood `quranic:root_001533:B005/m01`; the womb and discharge following the child `quranic:root_000994:B009/m01`; postpartum uterine pain or retained afterbirth `quranic:root_000552:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:1 `ٱلْقُرْءَانِ`; 50:10 `بَاسِقَٰتٍ`; 50:11 `أَحْيَيْنَا`; 50:16 `نَفْسُهُ`; 50:26 `ٱلْعَذَابِ`; 50:33 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنَ`
- Synthesis: New life is assembled in concealment before it crosses outward, with nourishment prepared in advance and bodily residue following emergence. This materially reframes the surah's disputed new creation: emergence is not an isolated leap from absence but the visible outcome of hidden collection, formation, provisioning, and release.

### S4. Whole-surah: Lots, Overseer, and Divided Camel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Gambling arrows are gathered in a leather holder, administered by an overseer, drawn as ranked lots, and used to assign winning and losing portions of a slaughtered camel.
- Active motifs: a leather holder gathering the arrows of the lot game `quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`; the game's overseer and third arrow `quranic:root_000584:B006/m01`; the fifth gambling arrow `quranic:root_001533:B016/m01`; the losing lot and what gambling takes away `quranic:root_000458:B019/m01`; players slaughtering a camel and dividing its parts by arrows `quranic:root_001694:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:18 `رَقِيبٌ`; 50:21 `نَفْسٍ`; 50:27 `رَبَّ`; 50:40 `أَدْبَٰرَ`; 50:44 `يَسِيرٌ`
- Synthesis: The practice assigns material shares through a concealed draw whose ranked arrows produce winners and losers. It usefully pressures the surah's judgment scenes by contrast: the preserved record, prior warning, and unalterable word do not allocate outcomes by chance, nor can responsibility be exchanged like a portion produced by the lots.

### S5. Whole-surah: Burial as Deliberate Concealment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A realized death is followed by turning the body onto its right side, wrapping or hiding it, placing it in a grave, and leaving it in a settled resting place.
- Active motifs: one realized state or instance of death `quranic:root_001454:B008/m01`; laying the dead on the right side `quranic:root_001698:B007/m01`; concealing the dead in a shroud or grave `quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`; hiding someone by burial in a grave `quranic:root_001117:B009/m01`; the grave as a settled resting place `quranic:root_000897:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:3 `مِتْنَا`; 50:9 `جَنَّٰتٍ`; 50:17 `ٱلْيَمِينِ`; 50:33 `بِٱلْغَيْبِ`; 50:43 `ٱلْمَصِيرُ`
- Synthesis: Burial appears as an intentional sequence of orientation, covering, placement, and settlement rather than mere disappearance into earth. This materializes the interval between death and gathering, so the earth's later splitting reverses a deliberate human act of concealment and sharpens the surah's claim that hidden bodies remain recoverable.

### S6. Whole-surah: Arrow Balance and Missing the Target
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An arrow is fitted to the string, its feathers determine its balance and lightness, and defects or excess trajectory carry it beyond and behind the intended target.
- Active motifs: an arrow's nock and its placement on the bowstring `quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`; fine arrow feathers or a light arrow `quranic:root_000324:B006/m02`; weak or misaligned arrow feathers `quranic:root_001360:B002/m01`; an arrow rising beyond its target `quranic:root_000945:B010/m01`; an arrow passing or falling behind the target `quranic:root_000458:B018/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:6 `فَوْقَهُمْ`; 50:10 `طَلْعٌ`; 50:38 `لُّغُوبٍ`; 50:40 `أَدْبَٰرَ`; 50:44 `حَشْرٌ`
- Synthesis: Direction depends on fitted components: an arrow can be light and balanced, weakened by bad feathers, or driven past its mark. The scene usefully pressures the surah's directional claims by contrast, since death, the cry, warning, and judgment reach their appointed subjects without the deviation, defect, or overshoot that can spoil a human shot.

### S7. P1-P2-P3 Role Progression: Contested Truth Becomes Effective Event
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: P1 to P2 to P3. Bridge: a repeated truth-bearing scene signature changes the role of truth from disputed content, to an encountered condition, to a causally effective signal.
- Active motifs: truth matching reality `quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`; truth becoming due or binding `quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`; denying something as a lie `quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`; arrival `quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`; a terrifying cry `quranic:root_000895:B002/m01`; bringing what is hidden outward `quranic:root_000400:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: P1, 50:5 `كَذَّبُوا بِٱلْحَقِّ`; P2, 50:19 `جَآءَتْ سَكْرَةُ ٱلْمَوْتِ بِٱلْحَقِّ`; P3, 50:42 `يَسْمَعُونَ ٱلصَّيْحَةَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ذَٰلِكَ يَوْمُ ٱلْخُرُوجِ`
- Synthesis: In P1, truth can still be treated as an object of speech and called false. In P2, the same truth-marker qualifies death's arrival, so truth is no longer merely argued about but encountered as a condition. In P3, it qualifies a cry whose result is outward emergence. The bridge is this increase in causal force: denial cannot keep truth at the level of contested discourse because the surah progressively gives it event, sound, and material outcome.

### S8. P1-P3 Reversal: Earth Changes from Subtractor to Releaser
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: P1 to P3. Bridge: the same material setting undergoes a role reversal, first diminishing what is placed within it and later opening to release a gathered population.
- Active motifs: earth as the lower material field `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; loss or subtraction `quranic:root_001542:B001/m01`; splitting or opening `quranic:root_000807:B001/m01`; outward passage `quranic:root_000400:B001/m01`; gathering a group to a destination `quranic:root_000324:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: P1, 50:4 `تَنقُصُ ٱلْأَرْضُ`; P3, 50:42 `يَوْمُ ٱلْخُرُوجِ`; P3, 50:44 `تَشَقَّقُ ٱلْأَرْضُ عَنْهُمْ سِرَاعًا ذَٰلِكَ حَشْرٌ عَلَيْنَا يَسِيرٌ`
- Synthesis: P1 assigns earth an inward operation: it receives bodies and subtracts from their available matter. P3 reverses that operation without changing the medium: earth splits from around them, outward passage replaces diminution, and separated occupants become a gathered body. The bridge materializes resurrection as a change in the earth's function, from enclosing and reducing to opening and delivering.

### S9. P1-P2-P3 Causal Bridge: Warning Moves from Notice to Event to Reminder
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: P1 to P2 to P3. Bridge: warning follows a causal role progression from dispatched notice, to appointed event and prior judicial notice, then returns as present address that can awaken caution.
- Active motifs: a messenger or carried message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; a threat or warning `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`; an appointed time or place `quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`; breath sent into an instrument `quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`; a horn `quranic:root_000891:B004/m01`; advancing or preceding `quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`; a reminder that restores presence `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; awakening fear that prompts caution `quranic:root_000447:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: P1, 50:14 `كَذَّبَ ٱلرُّسُلَ فَحَقَّ وَعِيدِ`; P2, 50:20 `نُفِخَ فِى ٱلصُّورِ ذَٰلِكَ يَوْمُ ٱلْوَعِيدِ`; P2, 50:28 `قَدَّمْتُ إِلَيْكُم بِٱلْوَعِيدِ`; P3, 50:45 `فَذَكِّرْ بِٱلْقُرْءَانِ مَن يَخَافُ وَعِيدِ`
- Synthesis: P1 locates warning in the messenger-recipient relation and shows it becoming due through rejection. P2 gives that warning an appointed day, activates it through the horn, and treats its earlier delivery as prior notice in judgment. P3 turns the depicted future back toward the present hearer through reminder and caution. The bridge makes judgment neither accidental nor unannounced: notice precedes event, remains evidentially available, and is renewed before the hearer's response is fixed.

### S10. Whole-surah: Official Register, Inkwell, and Identifying Mark
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An identifying mark makes its bearer distinguishable, writing clarifies the intended identity, an inkwell supplies the pen, and a name is entered into an official register.
- Active motifs: a distinguishing mark or guiding sign `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; disclosure of meaning through writing, speech, or sign `quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`; entering a name in an official register or roll `quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`; an inkwell supplying a pen with ink `quranic:root_001407:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:4 `عَلِمْنَا`, `كِتَٰبٌ`; 50:7 `مَدَدْنَٰهَا`; 50:38 `بَيْنَهُمَا`
- Synthesis: The preserved record acquires a concrete production sequence: identity is marked, meaning is made legible, ink sustains inscription, and the name is assigned a stable place in the roll. This supports the surah's answer to material loss by showing how a record can preserve not merely undifferentiated data but a recoverable, individually identified subject.

### S11. Whole-surah: Written Surety and Personal Guarantee
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A guarantor remains attached to the guaranteed party, a written undertaking names the assumed obligation, and a recognized community representative stands as witness and surety.
- Active motifs: bearing a guarantee and remaining with the guaranteed party `quranic:root_000840:B003/m01`; guaranteeing another person through an undertaking or written bond `quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`; standing surety over a person `quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`; a community representative who serves as witness or guarantor `quranic:root_001539:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:27 `كَانَ`; 50:36 `قَبْلَ`, `نَقَّبُوا`; 50:39 `فَٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: Surety is a formal social mechanism, not mere companionship: obligation is expressly assumed, fixed in writing, and backed by a recognized representative. This usefully pressures the companion's defense in the judgment scene. Proximity alone does not create a valid transfer of liability, and the accused cannot acquire a guarantor after responsibility has already been fixed.

### S12. Whole-surah: Appraisal, Advance, and Bargained Price
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods reach a market, their value is appraised, a contract is bound, an advance is paid, and the buyer seeks a reduction in the agreed price.
- Active motifs: a marketplace where goods are brought for sale `quranic:root_000762:B005/m01`; valuation, appraisal, and pricing of goods `quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`; binding and strengthening a contract `quranic:root_000782:B001/m01`; an advance or prepaid price in a sale `quranic:root_000737:B005/m01`; a buyer asking that the price be reduced `quranic:root_001542:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:4 `تَنقُصُ`; 50:14 `قَوْمُ`; 50:21 `سَآئِقٌ`; 50:26 `شَّدِيدِ`; 50:34 `سَلَٰمٍ`
- Synthesis: The transaction allows value to be assessed, terms secured, payment advanced, and price contested. It therefore sharpens the surah's judicial contrast: the final word is not a market quotation, the assigned outcome is not open to bargaining, and no late request can discount or substitute what the record has made due.

### S13. Whole-surah: Hidden Hunter, Heart Strike, and Verification
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter goes out, watches unseen from a blind, strikes the quarry at the heart, and then inspects it to determine whether death has occurred.
- Active motifs: going out in pursuit of game `quranic:root_000745:B006/m01`; a screen behind which a hunter or archer hides `quranic:root_000584:B011/m01`; striking or being struck in the heart `quranic:root_001248:B016/m01`; inspecting wounded game to verify whether it has died `quranic:root_001454:B014/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:6 `ٱلسَّمَآءِ`; 50:18 `رَقِيبٌ`; 50:33 `قَلْبٍ`; 50:43 `نُمِيتُ`
- Synthesis: The quarry's inability to see the concealed watcher does not interrupt observation, impact, or verification. This role structure usefully pressures the surah's heedlessness and surveillance scenes: being unaware of the watcher is not being unwatched, and death is an observed outcome rather than an uncertainty that permits escape.

### S14. Whole-surah: Main Vessel, Tender, and Anchor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A chief sailor directs a pitched vessel, a small tender remains available for its needs, and an anchor arrests the movement of the larger craft.
- Active motifs: the chief of a ship's sailors `quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`; a pitched or tar-coated ship `quranic:root_000973:B005/m01`; a small tender accompanying a larger vessel `quranic:root_001212:B011/m01`; a ship's mooring and anchor `quranic:root_000564:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:7 `رَوَٰسِىَ`; 50:8 `عَبْدٍ`; 50:16 `أَقْرَبُ`; 50:27 `رَبَّ`
- Synthesis: The maritime assembly distinguishes propulsion and support from the operation that ends drift. Captain and tender sustain navigation, but the anchor changes a moving vessel into a fixed one. This materializes the surah's mountains as stabilizers: their firmness is not inert mass alone but an anchoring function that holds an extended earth in place.

### S15. Whole-surah: Night Watering, Satiety, and Return
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A herd travels toward water, reaches the watering place, drinks while water is drawn at its mouths, fills to satiety, gives a thirsty animal another turn, and moves to nearby pasture before returning.
- Active motifs: night travel or urgent movement toward a watering place `quranic:root_001212:B008/m01`; arrival at water or the route to a watering place `quranic:root_001640:B001/m01`; drawing water at the mouths of drinking camels `quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`; drinking or filling a waterskin to satiety `quranic:root_000286:B006/m01`; returning a thirsty camel to the trough for another drink `quranic:root_000464:B007/m01`; moving from water to nearby pasture and then returning `quranic:root_001486:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:9 `حَبَّ`; 50:12 `قَبْلَهُمْ`; 50:16 `أَقْرَبُ`, `ٱلْوَرِيدِ`; 50:34 `ٱدْخُلُوهَا`; 50:41 `يُنَادِ`
- Synthesis: Water provision becomes a regulated cycle of approach, arrival, filling, accommodation, grazing, and return. This supports the surah's rain and recurrence readings by showing provision sustaining movement beyond the first drink. It also contrasts with Hell's unending request for more: embodied creatures can reach satiety and re-enter a life cycle, while the consuming vessel cannot complete its intake.

### S16. Whole-surah: Spark, Bellows, and Growing Flame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fire-bearing wood is struck, weak sparks appear, bellows direct air into the ignition, and the fire develops into an intense flame.
- Active motifs: weak sparks that give little usable fire `quranic:root_000286:B011/m01`; wood suited to striking and producing abundant fire `quranic:root_001398:B005/m01`; blowing air into a fire with bellows `quranic:root_001529:B001/m02`; a bright, intense, or smokeless flame `quranic:root_001411:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 50:1 `مَجِيدِ`; 50:5 `مَّرِيجٍ`; 50:9 `حَبَّ`; 50:20 `نُفِخَ`
- Synthesis: Combustion advances through material and directed input: suitable wood yields sparks, air feeds them, and flame intensifies. This materially reframes Hell's request for more as the operating appetite of fire, while the bellows sense of directed breath resonates with the horn blast as another moment when an otherwise latent process is activated by air.
